Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on geophysics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 899. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 709 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by geophysics generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 709. QED.
